```json
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "476 dengan sisa 4",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1717136",
        "/document/content/section/note/exercise/solution/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "539 dengan sisa 7",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1378267",
        "/document/content/section/note/exercise/solution/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Bagilah, lalu periksa dengan mengalikan:",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id2601370",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/problem/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Langkah pembagian bersusun 1.461 dengan 13. Bagi 14 dengan 13 untuk memperoleh angka hasil bagi 1 dan sisa 1. Turunkan 6, bagi 16 dengan 13 untuk memperoleh 1 dan sisa 3. Turunkan 1, bagi 31 dengan 13 untuk memperoleh 2 dan sisa 5. Hasilnya 112 dengan sisa 5; pemeriksaan 112 dikali 13 lalu ditambah 5 menghasilkan 1.461.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-708",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table",
        "aria-label"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Pertama-tama kita mencoba membagi 1 dengan 13. Karena hal itu tidak dapat dilakukan, kita mencoba membagi 14 dengan 13.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-708",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Ada satu buah 13 dalam 14. Kita menulis 1 di atas 4.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-708",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/newline",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Pembagian bersusun 1.461 dengan 13; angka hasil bagi 1 ditulis di atas garis pembagian.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168288619539",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/media",
        "alt"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Kalikan 1 dengan 13 dan kurangkan hasil kali ini dari 14.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-708",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Pada langkah pertama, 13 dikurangkan dari 14 sehingga sisanya 1; angka hasil bagi di atasnya adalah 1.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168288619559",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/media",
        "alt"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Sekarang turunkan 6 dan ulangi langkah-langkah tersebut. Ada satu buah 13 dalam 16.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-708",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Tulis 1 di atas 6. Kalikan 1 dengan 13 dan kurangkan hasil kali ini dari 16.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-708",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/newline",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Pembagian bersusun 1.461 dengan 13 yang belum selesai; hasil bagi sementara 11 dengan sisa 3.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168288619580",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/media",
        "alt"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Sekarang turunkan 1 dan ulangi langkah-langkah tersebut. Ada dua buah 13 dalam 31.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-708",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Tulis 2 di atas 1. Kalikan 2 dengan 13 dan kurangkan hasil kali ini dari 31. Tidak ada lagi digit yang perlu diturunkan, jadi kita selesai.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-708",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/newline",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Sisanya adalah 5. Hasil",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-708",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/newline",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "adalah 112 dengan sisa 5.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-708",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/math",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Langkah terakhir pembagian bersusun 1.461 dengan 13 menunjukkan hasil 112 dengan sisa 5.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168288599518",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/media",
        "alt"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Pemeriksaan pembagian: hasil bagi 112 dikalikan pembagi 13, lalu sisa 5 ditambahkan sehingga diperoleh bilangan semula, 1.461.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168286094929",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/media",
        "alt"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "114 dengan sisa 11",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id2908889",
        "/document/content/section/note/exercise/solution/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "121 dengan sisa 9",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1781870",
        "/document/content/section/note/exercise/solution/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Bagilah dan periksa dengan mengalikan:",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id2219998",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/problem/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Langkah pembagian bersusun 74.521 dengan 241. Bagi 745 dengan 241 untuk memperoleh angka hasil bagi 3 dan sisa 22. Turunkan 2; karena 222 lebih kecil daripada 241, tulis angka hasil bagi 0. Turunkan 1 sehingga terbentuk 2.221, lalu bagi dengan 241 untuk memperoleh 9 dan sisa 52. Hasilnya 309 dengan sisa 52; pemeriksaan menghasilkan 74.521.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table",
        "aria-label"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Pertama-tama kita mencoba membagi 7 dengan 241. Karena hal itu tidak dapat dilakukan, kita mencoba 74, lalu 745. Karena 2 masuk ke dalam 7 sebanyak tiga kali, kita mencoba 3.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Karena",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/newline",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": ", kita menulis 3 di atas digit 5 pada 745.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/math",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Perhatikan bahwa 4 terlalu besar karena",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/newline",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": ", yang lebih besar daripada 745.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/math",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Kalikan 3 dengan 241 dan kurangkan hasil kali ini dari 745.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Pada pembagian 74.521 dengan 241, tiga digit pertama 745 dibagi 241 sehingga angka hasil bagi 3 ditulis di atas garis. Tujuh ratus dua puluh tiga dikurangkan dari 745 dan menyisakan 22.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168287450666",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/media",
        "alt"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Sekarang turunkan 2 dan ulangi langkah-langkah tersebut. Dua ratus empat puluh satu tidak dapat masuk ke dalam 222.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Kita menulis 0 di atas 2 sebagai penanda tempat, lalu melanjutkan.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/newline",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Digit keempat, 2, diturunkan di samping sisa 22 sehingga terbentuk 222, yang lebih kecil daripada 241. Karena itu, angka nol ditulis di atas garis pembagian.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168287450687",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/media",
        "alt"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Sekarang turunkan 1 dan ulangi langkah-langkah tersebut. Cobalah 9. Karena",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "kita menulis 9 di atas 1. Kalikan 9 dengan 241 dan kurangkan hasil kali ini dari 2.221.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/newline",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Pembagian bersusun 74.521 dengan 241 menghasilkan 309 dengan sisa 52.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168287450720",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/media",
        "alt"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Tidak ada lagi digit yang perlu diturunkan, jadi kita selesai. Sisanya adalah 52. Jadi, hasil",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "adalah 309 dengan sisa 52.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-787",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/newline",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Perkalian 309 dengan 241, kemudian ditambah sisa 52, menghasilkan 74.521.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168287450748",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry/media",
        "alt"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Kadang-kadang tidak langsung jelas berapa kali pembagi dapat masuk ke dalam digit-digit bilangan yang dibagi. Kita perlu mencoba dan memeriksa beberapa bilangan untuk menemukan bilangan terbesar yang dapat masuk tanpa melebihi digit-digit tersebut.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id2202222",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "307 dengan sisa 49",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1826518",
        "/document/content/section/note/exercise/solution/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "308 dengan sisa 77",
    "slots": [

        "fs-id1334647",
        "/document/content/section/note/exercise/solution/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Menerjemahkan Frasa Verbal ke Notasi Matematika",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id2594193",
        "/document/content/section/title",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Sebelumnya dalam bagian ini, kita menerjemahkan notasi matematika untuk pembagian ke dalam kata-kata. Sekarang kita akan menerjemahkan frasa verbal ke notasi matematika. Beberapa frasa yang menunjukkan pembagian disajikan pada",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1617036",
        "/document/content/section/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Tabel empat kolom yang menunjukkan operasi pembagian, frasa verbal yang menandakan pembagian, contoh dengan 12 dan 4, serta empat bentuk ekspresi: tanda bagi, pecahan, garis miring, dan kurung pembagian bersusun.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1619545",
        "/document/content/section/table",
        "aria-label"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"

  },
  {
    "source": "Kita akan menggunakan strategi yang sama seperti pada bagian-bagian sebelumnya untuk menyelesaikan soal penerapan. Pertama, kita tentukan apa yang dicari. Kemudian kita tulis frasa yang memuat informasi untuk menemukannya. Setelah itu, kita terjemahkan frasa tersebut ke notasi matematika dan menyederhanakannya untuk memperoleh jawaban. Terakhir, kita tulis sebuah kalimat untuk menjawab pertanyaan.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1467564",
        "/document/content/section/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Cecelia membeli sekotak besar havermut seberat",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1577532",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/problem/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "di toko grosir. Ia ingin membagi",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1577532",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/problem/para/math",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "160 ons avoirdupois (oz; 1 oz ≈ 28,35 gram)",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1577532",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/problem/para/math/mrow/mtext",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "oz havermut itu menjadi porsi-porsi seberat",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1577532",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/problem/para/math",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": ". Setiap porsi akan dimasukkannya ke dalam kantong plastik agar ia dapat membawa satu kantong ke tempat kerja setiap hari. Berapa porsi yang diperolehnya dari kotak besar itu?",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1577532",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/problem/para/math",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "8 oz",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1577532",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/problem/para/math/mtext",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Kita diminta menentukan banyaknya porsi yang akan diperolehnya dari kotak besar tersebut.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id2212160",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Menunjukkan proses menyelesaikan soal cerita pembagian: menulis frasa, menerjemahkannya ke notasi matematika, menyederhanakan, dan menyatakan jawaban akhir.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168287366351",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table",
        "summary"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Tulis sebuah frasa.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168287366351",
        "/document/content/section/example/exercise/solution/table/tgroup/tbody/row/entry",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },

    "slots": [

        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {

    "slots": [

        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },

        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Tulis sebuah kalimat untuk menjawab pertanyaan.",
    "slots": [

      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Cecelia akan memperoleh 20 porsi dari kotak besar itu.",

        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },

      ]
    ],

        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Andrea sedang membuat pita hias untuk dikenakan para siswi di kelas tarinya saat pertunjukan. Setiap pita hias memerlukan",

        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {

        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Jenna menggunakan 26 pasang lensa kontak, tetapi masih tersisa 1 hari, sehingga ia memerlukan 27 pasang untuk 365 hari.",

  },

        "text"
      ]
    ],

  },
  {
    "source": "Satu kantong makanan kucing cukup untuk memberi makan kucing Lara selama",

      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Matematika Sehari-hari",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1562530",
        "/document/content/section/section/title",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Jelaskan bagaimana fakta perkalian dapat digunakan untuk membantu melakukan pembagian.",

      [
        "fs-id1885749",
        "/document/content/section/section/exercise/problem/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Jawaban dapat bervariasi. Fakta perkalian dapat membantu Anda memeriksa jawaban setelah menyelesaikan pembagian.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id1756683",
        "/document/content/section/section/exercise/solution/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {

    "slots": [

        "/document/content/section/section/exercise/problem/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "dan mengatakan bahwa jawabannya adalah",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id2204763",
        "/document/content/section/section/exercise/problem/para/math",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": ". Bagaimana Anda dapat memeriksa bahwa jawabannya benar?",
    "slots": [
      [
        "fs-id2204763",
        "/document/content/section/section/exercise/problem/para/math",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Pemeriksaan Mandiri",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-148",
        "/document/content/section/section/title",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Setelah menyelesaikan latihan, gunakan daftar periksa ini untuk menilai penguasaan Anda atas tujuan bagian ini.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168469596677",
        "/document/content/section/section/para/span",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Daftar periksa penilaian mandiri dengan tiga kolom tanggapan—‘Ya—dengan yakin’, ‘Ya—dengan sedikit bantuan’, dan ‘Tidak—saya tidak mengerti’—serta lima baris tujuan: menggunakan notasi pembagian; memodelkan pembagian bilangan cacah; membagi bilangan cacah; menerjemahkan frasa verbal ke notasi matematika; dan membagi bilangan cacah dalam penerapan.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1164270856201",
        "/document/content/section/section/figure/media",
        "alt"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Secara keseluruhan, setelah melihat daftar periksa tersebut, apakah Anda merasa telah siap untuk bab berikutnya? Mengapa atau mengapa tidak?",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-id1168466075811",
        "/document/content/section/section/para/span",
        "tail"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {

    "slots": [

        "/document/content/section/title",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {

    "slots": [

        "/document/content/section/section/title/link",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },

        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {
    "source": "Dalam latihan berikut, tentukan bilangan mana yang merupakan (a) bilangan asli dan (b) bilangan cacah.",
    "slots": [
      [
        "eip-527",
        "/document/content/section/section/para",
        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },
  {

        "text"
      ]
    ],

  },

        "text"

        "tail"
      ]
    ],

        "text"
      ]
    ],
    "status": "explicit two-register phrase row"
  },

    "source": "Diagram blok nilai tempat menunjukkan dua blok ratusan, lima batang puluhan, dan delapan blok satuan; 200 + 50 + 8 = 258.",

  {
    "source": "Tabel penjumlahan dengan kepala baris dan kolom 0 sampai 9.   {

    "source": "Tabel perkalian dengan kepala baris dan kolom 0 sampai 9. ## Reproducible read-only validation probe
```

This code reads only the two pinned source/review inputs and current draft. It does not run a build CLI or write any artifact. Math/image/source-answer review outside this probe is separately recorded above, not hidden in its success status.

```python
import sys,json,re,hashlib,copy,xml.etree.ElementTree as E
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,str(Path('languages/jv-Latn-ID/scripts').resolve()))
from draft_units import pinned_blob
from build import translated,XML_LANG
from build_units import tree_key
p=Path('languages/jv-Latn-ID/translation/a00-divide-whole.edits.json')
raw=p.read_bytes();ed=json.loads(raw)
root=E.fromstring(pinned_blob(ed['source_pins']['indonesian']|{'name':'a00-id'},ed['source_pins']['indonesian']['path']))
L=lambda n:n.tag.rsplit('}',1)[-1]
M='{http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML}'
allowed={'alt','aria-label','summary'}
source_keys=set()
for a in root.iter():
 for val in [a.text,a.tail]+[a.get(k) for k in allowed]:
  if val and re.search('[A-Za-z]',val.strip()):source_keys.add(val.strip())
rowkeys=[x[0] for x in ed['phrases']]
assert len(rowkeys)==len(set(rowkeys))
assert source_keys==set(rowkeys)|set(ed['unchanged_identifiers']),(source_keys-set(rowkeys)-set(ed['unchanged_identifiers']),set(rowkeys)-source_keys)
idset={n.get('id') for n in root.iter() if n.get('id')}
unresolved=[n.attrib for n in root.iter() if n.get('target-id') and not n.get('document') and n.get('target-id') not in idset]
assert not unresolved,unresolved
def validate(t):
 assert len(list(root.iter()))==len(list(t.iter()))
 assert t.get(XML_LANG)=='jv-Latn-ID'
 for a,b in zip(root.iter(),t.iter()):
  assert a.tag==b.tag and len(a)==len(b),'source tree'
  assert {k:v for k,v in a.attrib.items() if k not in allowed and k!=XML_LANG}=={k:v for k,v in b.attrib.items() if k not in allowed and k!=XML_LANG},'source attribute/ID/link'
  if a.tag.startswith(M) and L(a)!='mtext':
   assert a.text==b.text,'mathematical token'
  for x,y in [(a.text,b.text),(a.tail,b.tail)]+[(a.get(k),b.get(k)) for k in allowed]:
   x=x or '';y=y or ''
   assert re.findall(r'\d(?:[\d,.]*\d)?',x)==re.findall(r'\d(?:[\d,.]*\d)?',y),'number/grouping'
   assert re.findall(r'[$€£÷×+−=<>≤≥≈…]',x)==re.findall(r'[$€£÷×+−=<>≤≥≈…]',y),'nonlinguistic symbol'
  if L(a)=='mtext' and not re.search('[A-Za-z]',a.text or ''):
   assert a.text==b.text,'literal mtext'
checks={}
targets={}
for track in ed['tracks']:
 t=translated(root,track,ed);validate(t);targets[track]=t
 serial=E.tostring(t,encoding='utf-8',xml_declaration=True)
 canon=E.canonicalize(E.tostring(t,encoding='unicode'),strip_text=True,rewrite_prefixes=True).encode()
 assert serial==E.tostring(translated(root,track,ed),encoding='utf-8',xml_declaration=True)
 checks[track]={'ordered_tree_all_attributes_ids_links_preserved':True,'exact_grouped_digit_tokens_preserved':True,'nonlinguistic_symbols_preserved':True,'non_mtext_math_exact':True,'nonlinguistic_mtext_exact':True,'deterministic_in_memory_replay':True,'serialization_bytes':len(serial),'serialization_sha256':hashlib.sha256(serial).hexdigest(),'canonical_bytes':len(canon),'canonical_sha256':hashlib.sha256(canon).hexdigest()}
t=targets['jv-academic']
probes={}
def reject(name,mutate):
 q=copy.deepcopy(t);mutate(q)
 try:validate(q)
 except AssertionError as e:probes[name]={'rejected':True,'reason':str(e)}
 else:raise AssertionError('mutation accepted: '+name)
reject('source_ID',lambda q:next(n for n in q.iter() if n.get('id')).set('id','mutated-source-ID'))
reject('operator',lambda q:setattr(next(q.iter(M+'mo')),'text','+'))
reject('operand',lambda q:setattr(next(q.iter(M+'mn')),'text','999'))
reject('currency_symbol',lambda q:setattr(next(n for n in q.iter(M+'mtext') if chr(36) in (n.text or '')),'text',next(n for n in q.iter(M+'mtext') if chr(36) in (n.text or '')).text.replace(chr(36),chr(8364))))
reject('grouped_number',lambda q:setattr(next(n for n in q.iter(M+'mn') if ',' in (n.text or '')),'text','5394'))
reject('link_target',lambda q:next(n for n in q.iter() if n.get('target-id')).set('target-id','nonexistent'))
reject('long_division_geometry_attribute',lambda q:next(n for n in q.iter(M+'menclose') if n.get('notation')=='longdiv').set('notation','box'))
def swap(q):
 n=next(n for n in q.iter(M+'mfrac') if len(n)==2 and E.tostring(n[0])!=E.tostring(n[1]))
 n[:]=[n[1],n[0]]
reject('fraction_operand_order',swap)
def remove(q):
 par=next(n for n in q.iter() if any(L(c)=='solution' for c in n))
 par.remove(next(c for c in par if L(c)=='solution'))
reject('missing_solution',remove)
bad=copy.deepcopy(ed);bad['phrases']=[r for r in bad['phrases'] if r[0]!='Membagi Bilangan Cacah']
try:translated(root,'jv-academic',bad)
except ValueError as e:probes['unmapped_source_phrase']={'rejected':True,'reason':str(e)}
else:raise AssertionError('missing key accepted')
canon_names=['para','sisa','turah','ping','gunggung','lima','atus','ewu','yuta','wolu','sanga','sewidak','tambah','suda','bunder','cedhak','jembar','dawa','amba']
canons=[]
for name in canon_names:
 path=Path('downloads/jv-Latn-ID/canon')/(name+'.txt');r=path.read_bytes()
 canons.append({'path':str(path).replace(chr(92),'/'),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(r).hexdigest(),'bytes':len(r)})
meta=next(n for n in root if L(n)=='metadata')
glossary=next(n for n in root if L(n)=='glossary')
mathrecords=[{'ordinal':i+1,'anchor_sha256':hashlib.sha256(tree_key(n).encode()).hexdigest()} for i,n in enumerate(root.iter(M+'math'))]
out={'file_sha256':hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),'file_bytes':len(raw),'phrase_rows':len(rowkeys),'invariant_keys':len(ed['unchanged_identifiers']),'all_source_linguistic_keys':len(source_keys),'checks':checks,'mutation_probes':probes,'canon_files':canons,'metadata_cnxml':E.tostring(meta,encoding='unicode'),'glossary_cnxml':E.tostring(glossary,encoding='unicode'),'math_tags':sorted({L(n) for n in root.iter() if n.tag.startswith(M)}),'links':[n.attrib for n in root.iter() if L(n)=='link'],'nonlinguistic_mtext':[n.text for n in root.iter(M+'mtext') if not re.search('[A-Za-z]',n.text or '')],'linguistic_mtext':[n.text for n in root.iter(M+'mtext') if re.search('[A-Za-z]',n.text or '')]}
print(json.dumps(out,ensure_ascii=True))
```
